Experiments on the \emph{General AI Assistants} benchmark and \emph{Humanity's Last Exam} demonstrate sustained gains, achieving 26.2\% and 116.2\% relative improvements in overall accuracy, respectively. Code is available at https://github.com/Memento-Teams/Memento-Skills.

</details>


### [155] [NeuroGame Transformer: Gibbs-Inspired Attention Driven by Game Theory and Statistical Physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18761)
*Djamel Bouchaffra,Fayçal Ykhlef,Hanene Azzag,Mustapha Lebbah,Bilal Faye*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: NGT将注意力机制重新概念化为合作博弈和统计物理系统，使用Shapley值和Banzhaf指数量化token重要性，通过Ising模型能量函数计算注意力权重，采用重要性加权蒙特卡洛估计实现可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统transformer的注意力机制受限于成对公式，难以建模token间的高阶依赖关系。需要一种新框架来捕捉更复杂的token交互模式。

Method: 将token视为合作博弈中的玩家和统计物理中的自旋。使用Shapley值（全局排列归因）和Banzhaf指数（局部联盟影响）量化重要性，通过可学习门控参数组合为外部磁场。成对交互势捕获协同关系，系统能量遵循Ising哈密顿量，注意力权重作为Gibbs分布的边际概率出现，通过平均场方程高效计算。为处理指数级联盟空间，开发了重要性加权蒙特卡洛估计器。

Result: 在SNLI和MNLI-matched数据集上表现优异，超越多个高效transformer基线。SNLI测试准确率达到86.4%（验证峰值86.6%），超过ALBERT-Base，与RoBERTa-Base竞争激烈。

Conclusion: NGT通过博弈论和统计物理的融合，成功建模了token间的高阶依赖关系，提供了一种理论严谨且计算可行的注意力机制替代方案，在自然语言理解任务中展现出竞争力。

Abstract: Standard attention mechanisms in transformers are limited by their pairwise formulation, which hinders the modeling of higher-order dependencies among tokens. We introduce the NeuroGame Transformer (NGT) to overcome this by reconceptualizing attention through a dual perspective: tokens are treated simultaneously as players in a cooperative game and as interacting spins in a statistical physics system. Token importance is quantified using two complementary game-theoretic concepts -- Shapley values for global, permutation-based attribution and Banzhaf indices for local, coalition-level influence. These are combined via a learnable gating parameter to form an external magnetic field, while pairwise interaction potentials capture synergistic relationships. The system's energy follows an Ising Hamiltonian, with attention weights emerging as marginal probabilities under the Gibbs distribution, efficiently computed via mean-field equations. To ensure scalability despite the exponential coalition space, we develop importance-weighted Monte Carlo estimators with Gibbs-distributed weights. This approach avoids explicit exponential factors, ensuring numerical stability for long sequences. We provide theoretical convergence guarantees and characterize the fairness-sensitivity trade-off governed by the interpolation parameter. Experimental results demonstrate that the NeuroGame Transformer achieves strong performance across SNLI, and MNLI-matched, outperforming some major efficient transformer baselines. On SNLI, it attains a test accuracy of 86.4\% (with a peak validation accuracy of 86.6\%), surpassing ALBERT-Base and remaining highly competitive with RoBERTa-Base. Code is available at https://github.com/dbouchaffra/NeuroGame-Transformer.

</details>


### [156] [A Concept is More Than a Word: Diversified Unlearning in Text-to-Image Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18767)
*Duc Hao Pham,Van Duy Truong,Duy Khanh Dinh,Tien Cuong Nguyen,Dien Hy Ngo,Tuan Anh Bui*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Diversified Unlearning方法，通过多样化提示而非单一关键词来更精确地表示和消除扩散模型中的有害概念，解决现有基于关键词的方法因概念多维度性和语义重叠导致的过遗忘问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于关键词的概念消除方法存在根本性局限：视觉概念是多维度的，可以通过多种文本形式表达，且在潜在空间中常与相关概念重叠。单一关键词仅代表概念的窄点估计，无法覆盖其完整语义分布和潜在空间中的纠缠变化，导致消除过程脆弱且容易过度遗忘相关概念。

Method: 提出Diversified Unlearning分布框架，通过一组上下文多样化的提示而非单一关键词来表示概念。这种更丰富的表示使消除过程更精确和鲁棒。该方法可作为附加组件集成到现有的消除流程中。

Result: 在多个基准测试和最先进基线上的广泛实验表明，将Diversified Unlearning作为附加组件集成到现有消除流程中，能一致实现：更强的概念消除效果、更好的无关概念保留能力、以及对抗恢复攻击的改进鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 通过多样化提示而非单一关键词来表示概念，能够更精确地定位目标概念，避免过度遗忘，为文本到图像扩散模型的概念消除提供了更有效和鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: Concept unlearning has emerged as a promising direction for reducing the risks of harmful content generation in text-to-image diffusion models by selectively erasing undesirable concepts from a model's parameters. Existing approaches typically rely on keywords to identify the target concept to be unlearned. However, we show that this keyword-based formulation is inherently limited: a visual concept is multi-dimensional, can be expressed in diverse textual forms, and often overlap with related concepts in the latent space, making keyword-only unlearning, which imprecisely indicate the target concept is brittle and prone to over-forgetting. This occurs because a single keyword represents only a narrow point estimate of the concept, failing to cover its full semantic distribution and entangled variations in the latent space. To address this limitation, we propose Diversified Unlearning, a distributional framework that represents a concept through a set of contextually diverse prompts rather than a single keyword. This richer representation enables more precise and robust unlearning. Through extensive experiments across multiple benchmarks and state-of-the-art baselines, we demonstrate that integrating Diversified Unlearning as an add-on component into existing unlearning pipelines consistently achieves stronger erasure, better retention of unrelated concepts, and improved robustness against adversarial recovery attacks.

</details>


### [157] [Proceedings of the 2nd Workshop on Advancing Artificial Intelligence through Theory of Mind](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18786)
*Nitay Alon,Joseph M. Barnby,Reuth Mirsky,Stefan Sarkadi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AAAI 2026研讨会论文集，聚焦心智理论在人工智能中的前沿研究


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为心智理论与人工智能研究社区提供开放获取的精选论文集，促进该交叉领域的发展与交流

Method: 通过研讨会形式征集论文，经过评审筛选后汇编成册，作为开放获取的学术资源

Result: 成功出版了包含多篇前沿研究的论文集，为ToM与AI社区提供了重要的学术参考资源

Conclusion: 该论文集代表了心智理论在人工智能领域的最新进展，为未来研究提供了基础框架和方向指引

Abstract: This volume includes a selection of papers presented at the 2nd Workshop on Advancing Artificial Intelligence through Theory of Mind held at AAAI 2026 in Singapore on 26th January 2026. The purpose of this volume is to provide an open access and curated anthology for the ToM and AI research community.

</details>


### [158] [dTRPO: Trajectory Reduction in Policy Optimization of Diffusion Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18806)
*Wenxuan Zhang,Lemeng Wu,Changsheng Zhao,Ernie Chang,Mingchen Zhuge,Zechun Liu,Andy Su,Hanxian Huang,Jun Chen,Chong Zhou,Raghuraman Krishnamoorthi,Vikas Chandra,Mohamed Elhoseiny,Wei Wen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出dTRPO方法，通过轨迹概率计算优化来提升扩散大语言模型的策略对齐效率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散大语言模型(dLLMs)为语言生成带来了新范式，但也带来了与人类偏好对齐的新挑战。现有方法在策略优化时轨迹概率计算成本高，限制了离线策略训练的扩展性。

Method: 提出轨迹缩减策略优化(dTRPO)：1) 证明在参考策略正则化下，新解掩码token的概率比是中间扩散状态概率比的无偏估计；2) 提出通过重新掩码最终状态的单次前向传递有效估计完整轨迹概率。将这两种轨迹缩减策略整合到策略优化目标中。

Result: 在7B参数dLLMs上评估，在指令跟随和推理基准上显著提升性能：STEM任务提升9.6%，编码任务提升4.3%，指令跟随任务提升3.0%。dTRPO具有离线、单次前向的高训练效率，并通过高质量输出提升生成效率。

Conclusion: dTRPO通过轨迹缩减策略有效解决了dLLMs策略优化中的计算瓶颈，实现了可扩展的离线策略训练，显著提升了模型在各种任务上的性能表现。

Abstract: Diffusion Large Language Models (dLLMs) introduce a new paradigm for language generation, which in turn presents new challenges for aligning them with human preferences. In this work, we aim to improve the policy optimization for dLLMs by reducing the cost of the trajectory probability calculation, thereby enabling scaled-up offline policy training. We prove that: (i) under reference policy regularization, the probability ratio of the newly unmasked tokens is an unbiased estimate of that of intermediate diffusion states, and (ii) the probability of the full trajectory can be effectively estimated with a single forward pass of a re-masked final state. By integrating these two trajectory reduction strategies into a policy optimization objective, we propose Trajectory Reduction Policy Optimization (dTRPO). We evaluate dTRPO on 7B dLLMs across instruction-following and reasoning benchmarks. Results show that it substantially improves the core performance of state-of-the-art dLLMs, achieving gains of up to 9.6% on STEM tasks, up to 4.3% on coding tasks, and up to 3.0% on instruction-following tasks. Moreover, dTRPO exhibits strong training efficiency due to its offline, single-forward nature, and achieves improved generation efficiency through high-quality outputs.

</details>


### [159] [Can LLM generate interesting mathematical research problems?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18813)
*Xiaoyang Chen,Xiang Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文是LLM数学创造力系列研究的第二篇，聚焦于探索LLM能否生成有价值的前沿数学研究问题，在微分几何领域生成了665个研究问题，其中许多对专家而言是未知且具有独特研究价值的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在前一篇论文建立了LLM数学创造力评估标准的基础上，本研究旨在深入探索LLM是否能够生成具有价值和前沿性的数学研究问题，特别是验证LLM在创造未知数学问题方面的能力。

Method: 开发了一个智能体来生成未知的数学问题，在微分几何领域生成了665个研究问题，并通过人类专家验证来评估这些问题的创新性和价值。

Result: 研究发现许多生成的数学问题对领域专家来说是未知的，并且具有独特的研究价值，这表明LLM确实能够生成有价值的前沿数学研究问题。

Conclusion: LLM在数学创造力方面表现出潜力，能够生成具有研究价值的未知数学问题，这为LLM在数学研究领域的应用开辟了新的可能性。

Abstract: This paper is the second one in a series of work on the mathematical creativity of LLM. In the first paper, the authors proposed three criteria for evaluating the mathematical creativity of LLM and constructed a benchmark dataset to measure it. This paper further explores the mathematical creativity of LLM, with a focus on investigating whether LLM can generate valuable and cutting-edge mathematical research problems. We develop an agent to generate unknown problems and produced 665 research problems in differential geometry. Through human verification, we find that many of these mathematical problems are unknown to experts and possess unique research value.

</details>


### [160] [ProRL Agent: Rollout-as-a-Service for RL Training of Multi-Turn LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18815)
*Hao Zhang,Mingjie Liu,Shaokun Zhang,Songyang Han,Jian Hu,Zhenghui Jin,Yuchi Zhang,Shizhe Diao,Ximing Lu,Binfeng Xu,Zhiding Yu,Jan Kautz,Yi Dong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ProRL Agent是一个基于"rollout-as-a-service"理念的可扩展基础设施，通过API服务提供完整的智能体rollout生命周期管理，支持在无根HPC设置中进行多任务RL训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多轮LLM智能体在解决复杂交互任务中日益重要，RL是改进其长视野行为的关键。但现有基础设施通常将rollout编排与训练循环耦合，导致系统难以迁移和维护，且RL训练需要生成大量沙盒化rollout轨迹。

Method: 提出ProRL Agent基础设施，采用rollout-as-a-service理念，通过API服务提供完整的智能体rollout生命周期管理。提供标准化且可扩展的沙盒环境，支持在无根HPC设置中进行多样化的智能体任务。

Result: 在软件工程、数学、STEM和编程任务上验证了ProRL Agent的RL训练效果。该系统已开源并集成到NVIDIA NeMo Gym中。

Conclusion: ProRL Agent提供了一个可扩展、易于维护的基础设施，解决了现有RL训练系统中rollout编排与训练循环耦合的问题，支持多任务智能体训练，并已成功集成到工业级框架中。

Abstract: Multi-turn LLM agents are increasingly important for solving complex, interactive tasks, and reinforcement learning (RL) is a key ingredient for improving their long-horizon behavior. However, RL training requires generating large numbers of sandboxed rollout trajectories, and existing infrastructures often couple rollout orchestration with the training loop, making systems hard to migrate and maintain. Under the rollout-as-a-service philosophy, we present ProRL Agent , a scalable infrastructure that serves the full agentic rollout lifecycle through an API service. ProRL Agent also provides standardized and extensible sandbox environments that support diverse agentic tasks in rootless HPC settings. We validate ProRL Agent through RL training on software engineering, math, STEM, and coding tasks. ProRL Agent is open-sourced and integrated as part of NVIDIA NeMo Gym.

</details>


### [161] [RewardFlow: Topology-Aware Reward Propagation on State Graphs for Agentic RL with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18859)
*Xiao Feng,Bo Han,Zhanke Zhou,Jiaqi Fan,Jiangchao Yao,Ka Ho Li,Dahai Yu,Michael Kwok-Po Ng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RewardFlow是一种轻量级方法，通过构建状态图分析推理轨迹中的状态拓扑结构，量化状态对成功的贡献，为智能体推理任务提供密集的状态级奖励，显著提升RL性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习能增强大语言模型在外部环境中的智能体推理能力，但终端奖励的稀疏性阻碍了细粒度的状态级优化。虽然过程奖励建模是替代方案，但训练专用奖励模型计算成本高且难以扩展。

Method: RewardFlow利用推理轨迹中状态的内在拓扑结构，构建状态图分析状态对成功的贡献，通过拓扑感知的图传播量化贡献，生成客观的状态级奖励作为密集奖励用于RL优化。

Result: RewardFlow在四个智能体推理基准测试中显著优于先前的RL基线方法，表现出更优的性能、鲁棒性和训练效率。

Conclusion: RewardFlow通过轻量级的状态级奖励估计方法，有效解决了智能体推理任务中奖励稀疏性问题，为RL优化提供了密集、客观的奖励信号，显著提升了性能表现。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) holds significant promise for enhancing the agentic reasoning capabilities of large language models (LLMs) with external environments. However, the inherent sparsity of terminal rewards hinders fine-grained, state-level optimization. Although process reward modeling offers a promising alternative, training dedicated reward models often entails substantial computational costs and scaling difficulties. To address these challenges, we introduce RewardFlow, a lightweight method for estimating state-level rewards tailored to agentic reasoning tasks. RewardFlow leverages the intrinsic topological structure of states within reasoning trajectories by constructing state graphs. This enables an analysis of state-wise contributions to success, followed by topology-aware graph propagation to quantify contributions and yield objective, state-level rewards. When integrated as dense rewards for RL optimization, RewardFlow substantially outperforms prior RL baselines across four agentic reasoning benchmarks, demonstrating superior performance, robustness, and training efficiency. The implementation of RewardFlow is publicly available at https://github.com/tmlr-group/RewardFlow.

</details>


### [162] [Conflict-Based Search for Multi Agent Path Finding with Asynchronous Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18866)
*Xuemian Wu,Shizhe Zhao,Zhongqiang Ren*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CBS-AA方法解决异步动作多智能体路径规划问题，克服了CCBS的不完整性缺陷，保证完备性和最优性，并通过冲突消解技术将分支数减少达90%


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统MAPF算法依赖同步动作假设，限制了实际应用。CCBS方法虽能处理异步动作MAPF-AA，但最近被发现因连续等待时间导致状态空间无限而不完备，需要新的解决方案。

Method: 提出Conflict-Based Search with Asynchronous Actions (CBS-AA)方法，通过新的算法设计绕过CCBS的理论缺陷。基于CBS-AA开发冲突消解技术进一步改善可扩展性。

Result: CBS-AA能够解决MAPF-AA问题并保证完备性和解的最优性。测试结果显示，该方法能将分支数减少高达90%，显著提升算法性能。

Conclusion: CBS-AA成功解决了CCBS的不完整性缺陷，为异步动作多智能体路径规划提供了具有完备性和最优性保证的有效方法，并通过冲突消解技术大幅提升了算法效率。

Abstract: Multi-Agent Path Finding (MAPF) seeks collision-free paths for multiple agents from their respective start locations to their respective goal locations while minimizing path costs. Most existing MAPF algorithms rely on a common assumption of synchronized actions, where the actions of all agents start at the same time and always take a time unit, which may limit the use of MAPF planners in practice. To get rid of this assumption, Continuous-time Conflict-Based Search (CCBS) is a popular approach that can find optimal solutions for MAPF with asynchronous actions (MAPF-AA). However, CCBS has recently been identified to be incomplete due to an uncountably infinite state space created by continuous wait durations. This paper proposes a new method, Conflict-Based Search with Asynchronous Actions (CBS-AA), which bypasses this theoretical issue and can solve MAPF-AA with completeness and solution optimality guarantees. Based on CBS-AA, we also develop conflict resolution techniques to improve the scalability of CBS-AA further. Our test results show that our method can reduce the number of branches by up to 90%.

</details>


### [163] [Geography According to ChatGPT -- How Generative AI Represents and Reasons about Geography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18881)
*Krzysztof Janowicz,Gengchen Mai,Rui Zhu,Song Gao,Zhangyu Wang,Yingjie Hu,Lauren Bennett*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文探讨AI系统如何表示和推理地理知识，强调理解AI构建的世界观与评估其准确性同等重要，通过三个探索性案例说明研究必要性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着公众越来越多地通过AI系统与空间和地点互动，理解AI如何表示和推理地理知识至关重要。同时，研究本身也依赖预训练模型，因此理解AI构建的世界观与评估其事实回忆准确性同等重要

Method: 提出三个探索性案例（vignettes）作为研究动机：1）模型是否形成强默认假设，以及模型输出对微小语法变化的脆弱性；2）分布偏移是否可能从个体良性任务的组合中重新出现；3）当仅关注系统回忆地理事实的能力时，是否忽视了更深层次的理解问题

Result: 论文未提供具体实验结果，而是通过三个案例说明需要进一步研究的问题：模型默认假设的稳定性、任务组合引发的分布偏移风险、以及超越事实回忆的深层理解评估

Conclusion: 理解AI系统如何表示和推理地理知识是重要研究课题，需要超越传统准确性评估，关注AI构建的世界观、模型行为的稳定性以及深层理解能力

Abstract: Understanding how AI will represent and reason about geography should be a key concern for all of us, as the broader public increasingly interacts with spaces and places through these systems. Similarly, in line with the nature of foundation models, our own research often relies on pre-trained models. Hence, understanding what world AI systems construct is as important as evaluating their accuracy, including factual recall. To motivate the need for such studies, we provide three illustrative vignettes, i.e., exploratory probes, in the hope that they will spark lively discussions and follow-up work: (1) Do models form strong defaults, and how brittle are model outputs to minute syntactic variations? (2) Can distributional shifts resurface from the composition of individually benign tasks, e.g., when using AI systems to create personas? (3) Do we overlook deeper questions of understanding when solely focusing on the ability of systems to recall facts such as geographic principles?

</details>


### [164] [Quantitative Introspection in Language Models: Tracking Internal States Across Conversation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18893)
*Nicolas Martorell*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LLM的数值自我报告可作为追踪内部情感状态的有效工具，通过logit-based方法能揭示模型的内省能力，且这种能力随模型规模增大而增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前追踪大语言模型内部状态的方法（如线性探针）存在局限性：高维表示压缩不完美，且随模型规模增大应用难度增加。受人类心理学中数值自我报告的启发，研究探索LLM自身的数值自我报告是否能追踪探针定义的情感状态。

Method: 研究四个概念对（幸福感、兴趣、专注度、冲动性）在40个十轮对话中的表现，将内省操作化为模型自我报告与概念匹配探针定义内部状态之间的因果信息耦合。发现贪婪解码的自我报告输出有限值，但通过计算基于logit的自我报告能揭示内省能力。

Result: 基于logit的自我报告能追踪可解释的内部状态（Spearman ρ=0.40-0.76；等渗R²=0.12-0.54），随时间变化跟踪状态变化，激活导向确认耦合是因果性的。内省在对话第一轮就存在但随对话演化，可通过沿一个概念导向选择性提升另一概念的内省能力（ΔR²最高达0.30）。这些现象在某些情况下随模型规模扩展，在LLaMA-3.1-8B-Instruct中R²≈0.93。

Conclusion: 数值自我报告是追踪对话AI系统内部情感状态的可行补充工具，为模型安全、可解释性和模型福利提供了新方法。

Abstract: Tracking the internal states of large language models across conversations is important for safety, interpretability, and model welfare, yet current methods are limited. Linear probes and other white-box methods compress high-dimensional representations imperfectly and are harder to apply with increasing model size. Taking inspiration from human psychology, where numeric self-report is a widely used tool for tracking internal states, we ask whether LLMs' own numeric self-reports can track probe-defined emotive states over time. We study four concept pairs (wellbeing, interest, focus, and impulsivity) in 40 ten-turn conversations, operationalizing introspection as the causal informational coupling between a model's self-report and a concept-matched probe-defined internal state. We find that greedy-decoded self-reports collapse outputs to few uninformative values, but introspective capacity can be unmasked by calculating logit-based self-reports. This metric tracks interpretable internal states (Spearman $ρ= 0.40$-$0.76$; isotonic $R^2 = 0.12$-$0.54$ in LLaMA-3.2-3B-Instruct), follows how those states change over time, and activation steering confirms the coupling is causal. Furthermore, we find that introspection is present at turn 1 but evolves through conversation, and can be selectively improved by steering along one concept to boost introspection for another ($ΔR^2$ up to $0.30$). Crucially, these phenomena scale with model size in some cases, approaching $R^2 \approx 0.93$ in LLaMA-3.1-8B-Instruct, and partially replicate in other model families. Together, these results position numeric self-report as a viable, complementary tool for tracking internal emotive states in conversational AI systems.

</details>


### [165] [Secure Linear Alignment of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18908)
*Matt Gorbett,Suman Jana*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出隐私保护框架，利用语言模型表征收敛特性实现独立模型间的跨模型推理，通过仿射变换和同态加密保护查询隐私，实现亚秒级推理延迟


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型在不同训练目标、架构和数据模态下学习到相似表征，这种表征收敛为跨模型对齐下游任务创造了机会。在安全、隐私或竞争限制禁止直接数据或模型共享的场景下，需要隐私保护的跨模型推理方案

Method: 提出隐私保护框架：1) 在共享公共数据集上学习仿射变换对齐独立模型表征；2) 应用同态加密保护客户端查询；3) 仅加密线性对齐和分类操作以降低延迟。同时通过实证研究验证表征收敛，学习独立模型最终隐藏状态间的线性变换

Result: 1) 框架实现亚秒级推理延迟并保持强安全保证；2) 跨模型映射在嵌入分类和分布外检测上性能下降最小；3) 首次展示线性对齐有时能实现独立训练模型间的文本生成

Conclusion: 利用语言模型表征收敛特性，提出的隐私保护框架成功实现了独立模型间的安全跨模型推理，为禁止直接数据/模型共享的场景提供了可行解决方案，同时保持了实用性能

Abstract: Language models increasingly appear to learn similar representations, despite differences in training objectives, architectures, and data modalities. This emerging compatibility between independently trained models introduces new opportunities for cross-model alignment to downstream objectives. Moreover, it unlocks new potential application domains, such as settings where security, privacy, or competitive constraints prohibit direct data or model sharing. In this work, we propose a privacy-preserving framework that exploits representational convergence to enable cross-silo inference between independent language models. The framework learns an affine transformation over a shared public dataset and applies homomorphic encryption to protect client queries during inference. By encrypting only the linear alignment and classification operations, the method achieves sub-second inference latency while maintaining strong security guarantees. We support this framework with an empirical investigation into representational convergence, in which we learn linear transformations between the final hidden states of independent models. We evaluate these cross-model mappings on embedding classification and out-of-distribution detection, observing minimal performance degradation across model pairs. Additionally, we show for the first time that linear alignment sometimes enables text generation across independently trained models.

</details>


### [166] [Teleological Inference in Structural Causal Models via Intentional Interventions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18968)
*Dario Compagno,Fabio Massimo Zennaro*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种新方法，将结构因果模型（SCMs）扩展到目的论问题，通过引入"意向干预"操作符和结构最终模型（SFMs）来检测智能体及其意图


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统结构因果模型主要用于因果推断，但无法有效处理具有状态感知和目标导向的智能体在因果系统中的干预行为。现有方法在建模这类智能体及其意图方面存在局限性，需要新的理论框架来分析和理解智能体的目的性行为

Method: 引入"意向干预"这一时间无关的操作符，构建结构最终模型（SFMs）。SFMs将观测值视为意向干预的结果，并将其与干预的反事实条件（如果智能体不干预会发生什么）联系起来。该方法允许从观测数据中推断智能体的存在及其意图

Result: 提出的结构最终模型能够：1）经验性地检测因果系统中是否存在智能体；2）发现智能体的具体意图。SFMs为分析目的性行为提供了形式化框架，将因果推理扩展到目的论领域

Conclusion: 结构因果模型不仅可以处理传统因果问题，还能通过引入意向干预和结构最终模型来形式化和回答目的论问题。这一扩展为理解智能体在因果系统中的目标导向行为提供了新的理论工具，在人工智能、认知科学和复杂系统分析中具有应用潜力

Abstract: Structural causal models (SCMs) were conceived to formulate and answer causal questions. This paper shows that SCMs can also be used to formulate and answer teleological questions, concerning the intentions of a state-aware, goal-directed agent intervening in a causal system. We review limitations of previous approaches to modeling such agents, and then introduce intentional interventions, a new time-agnostic operator that induces a twin SCM we call a structural final model (SFM). SFMs treat observed values as the outcome of intentional interventions and relate them to the counterfactual conditions of those interventions (what would have happened had the agent not intervened). We show how SFMs can be used to empirically detect agents and to discover their intentions.

</details>


### [167] [Evaluating Game Difficulty in Tetris Block Puzzle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18994)
*Chun-Jui Wang,Jian-Ting Guo,Hung Guei,Chung-Chin Shih,Ti-Rong Wu,I-Chen Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 使用Stochastic Gumbel AlphaZero评估Tetris Block Puzzle变体难度，发现增加持有块和预览块降低难度，添加更多方块变体增加难度，T-五连方块造成最大减速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Tetris Block Puzzle及其变体拥有数千万下载量，但缺乏对不同规则集难度的系统性评估。需要一种原则性的方法来评估哪些规则集更难，为随机拼图游戏设计提供参考。

Method: 使用Stochastic Gumbel AlphaZero作为随机环境中的预算感知规划智能体，评估规则变化对难度的影响。评估的规则包括：持有块数量h、预览持有块数量p，以及额外的Tetris方块变体。使用训练奖励和收敛迭代次数作为难度度量指标。

Result: 增加h和p会降低难度（获得更高奖励和更快收敛），而添加更多Tetris方块变体会增加难度。其中T-五连方块产生的减速效应最大。SGAZ在小模拟预算下表现出强大性能，能够高效、可重复地比较不同规则集。

Conclusion: SGAZ为随机拼图游戏提供了有效的难度评估框架，能够高效比较不同规则集，为未来游戏设计提供参考。增加持有和预览功能简化游戏，而增加方块复杂性则提高挑战性。

Abstract: Tetris Block Puzzle is a single player stochastic puzzle in which a player places blocks on an 8 x 8 grid to complete lines; its popular variants have amassed tens of millions of downloads. Despite this reach, there is little principled assessment of which rule sets are more difficult. Inspired by prior work that uses AlphaZero as a strong evaluator for chess variants, we study difficulty in this domain using Stochastic Gumbel AlphaZero (SGAZ), a budget-aware planning agent for stochastic environments. We evaluate rule changes including holding block h, preview holding block p, and additional Tetris block variants using metrics such as training reward and convergence iterations. Empirically, increasing h and p reduces difficulty (higher reward and faster convergence), while adding more Tetris block variants increases difficulty, with the T-pentomino producing the largest slowdown. Through analysis, SGAZ delivers strong play under small simulation budgets, enabling efficient, reproducible comparisons across rule sets and providing a reference for future design in stochastic puzzle games.

</details>


### [168] [Regret Bounds for Competitive Resource Allocation with Endogenous Costs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18999)
*Rui Chai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文研究了具有内生成本的在线资源分配问题，分析了三种分配范式在对抗性序列下的遗憾界限，并揭示了交互矩阵拓扑结构对计算-遗憾权衡的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于解决标准在线优化与内生成本资源分配之间的差异。传统在线优化假设成本是外生的，而实际模块化系统中各模块间的交互（合作与竞争）使得成本取决于整个分配向量，这构成了与部分可观测性不同的基本挑战。

Method: 方法包括：1) 分析三种分配范式：均匀分配（忽略成本）、门控分配（估计成本）和竞争性分配（通过交互反馈的乘性权重更新）；2) 在具有有界变化的对抗性序列下建立严格分离的遗憾界限；3) 研究交互矩阵W的拓扑结构（完全交互、稀疏拓扑、环状结构）对计算-遗憾权衡的影响。

Result: 主要结果：1) 遗憾界限严格分离：均匀分配Ω(T)，门控分配O(T^{2/3})，竞争性分配O(√(T log N))；2) 拓扑结构影响：完全交互（|E|=O(N²)）获得最紧界限但计算成本最高，稀疏拓扑（|E|=O(N)）最多增加O(√(log N))遗憾但将每步成本从O(N²)降至O(N)；3) 环状结构（如五行拓扑）最小化计算×遗憾乘积。

Conclusion: 结论：1) 首次为模块化架构中的去中心化竞争性分配提供了形式化的遗憾理论证明；2) 成本内生性是区别于部分可观测性的基本挑战；3) 交互拓扑结构决定了计算与遗憾之间的权衡，环状结构（如五行拓扑）在此权衡中达到最优。

Abstract: We study online resource allocation among N interacting modules over T rounds. Unlike standard online optimization, costs are endogenous: they depend on the full allocation vector through an interaction matrix W encoding pairwise cooperation and competition.
  We analyze three paradigms: (I) uniform allocation (cost-ignorant), (II) gated allocation (cost-estimating), and (III) competitive allocation via multiplicative weights update with interaction feedback (cost-revealing). Our main results establish a strict separation under adversarial sequences with bounded variation: uniform incurs Omega(T) regret, gated achieves O(T^{2/3}), and competitive achieves O(sqrt(T log N)). The performance gap stems from competitive allocation's ability to exploit endogenous cost information revealed through interactions.
  We further show that W's topology governs a computation-regret tradeoff. Full interaction (|E|=O(N^2)) yields the tightest bound but highest per-step cost, while sparse topologies (|E|=O(N)) increase regret by at most O(sqrt(log N)) while reducing per-step cost from O(N^2) to O(N). Ring-structured topologies with both cooperative and competitive links - of which the five-element Wuxing topology is canonical - minimize the computation x regret product.
  These results provide the first formal regret-theoretic justification for decentralized competitive allocation in modular architectures and establish cost endogeneity as a fundamental challenge distinct from partial observability.
  Keywords: online learning, regret bounds, resource allocation, endogenous costs, interaction topology, multiplicative weights, modular systems, Wuxing topology

</details>


### [169] [Behavioral Fingerprints for LLM Endpoint Stability and Identity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19022)
*Jonah Leshin,Manish Shah,Ian Timmis,Daniel Kang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Stability Monitor系统，用于监控AI模型端点的行为一致性，通过固定提示集采样输出并比较分布变化来检测模型更新、量化、推理栈等变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统可靠性指标（正常运行时间、延迟、吞吐量）无法捕捉模型行为变化，模型端点可能在保持"健康"状态时，由于权重、分词器、量化、推理引擎、内核、缓存、路由或硬件更新而导致有效模型身份发生变化。

Method: 引入Stability Monitor黑盒稳定性监控系统，周期性对端点进行指纹识别：使用固定提示集采样输出，比较随时间变化的输出分布。使用跨提示的求和能量距离统计量比较指纹，通过排列检验p值作为分布偏移证据，顺序聚合以检测变化事件并定义稳定期。

Result: 在受控验证中，Stability Monitor成功检测到模型系列、版本、推理栈、量化和行为参数的变化。在监控同一模型由多个提供商托管的真实场景中，观察到显著的提供商间和提供商内稳定性差异。

Conclusion: Stability Monitor提供了一种有效的方法来监控AI模型端点的行为一致性，弥补了传统可靠性指标的不足，能够检测各种可能导致模型身份变化的更新和配置变更。

Abstract: The consistency of AI-native applications depends on the behavioral consistency of the model endpoints that power them. Traditional reliability metrics such as uptime, latency and throughput do not capture behavioral change, and an endpoint can remain "healthy" while its effective model identity changes due to updates to weights, tokenizers, quantization, inference engines, kernels, caching, routing, or hardware. We introduce Stability Monitor, a black-box stability monitoring system that periodically fingerprints an endpoint by sampling outputs from a fixed prompt set and comparing the resulting output distributions over time. Fingerprints are compared using a summed energy distance statistic across prompts, with permutation-test p-values as evidence of distribution shift aggregated sequentially to detect change events and define stability periods. In controlled validation, Stability Monitor detects changes to model family, version, inference stack, quantization, and behavioral parameters. In real-world monitoring of the same model hosted by multiple providers, we observe substantial provider-to-provider and within-provider stability differences.

</details>


### [170] [Man and machine: artificial intelligence and judicial decision making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19042)
*Arthur Dyevre,Ahmad Shahvaroughi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对AI在司法决策中的应用进行了系统性综述，重点关注风险评估工具的性能、人类法官的偏见以及人机交互，指出当前AI对司法决策影响有限，但需要更多跨学科研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI技术在司法决策（特别是审前、量刑和假释）中的应用引发了透明度、可靠性和问责制的重大关切，同时凸显了人类判断的局限性，需要理解法官如何与AI决策辅助工具互动。

Method: 使用刑事司法风险评估作为焦点案例，进行综合性文献综述，连接AI工具性能与公平性、人类法官优势与偏见、以及人机交互三个相互关联的方面，涵盖计算机科学、经济学、法学、犯罪学和心理学等多个领域。

Result: 现有经验证据表明AI决策辅助工具对审前和量刑决策的影响有限或不存在，但在评估AI风险评估工具性能、理解法官在嘈杂决策环境中的行为以及个体特征如何影响法官对AI建议的反应方面存在重要研究空白。

Conclusion: AI与人类比较研究有潜力为算法工具和人类决策者提供新见解，倡导未来研究中进行更大的跨学科整合与交叉融合。

Abstract: The integration of artificial intelligence (AI) technologies into judicial decision-making - particularly in pretrial, sentencing, and parole contexts - has generated substantial concerns about transparency, reliability, and accountability. At the same time, these developments have brought the limitations of human judgment into sharper relief and underscored the importance of understanding how judges interact with AI-based decision aids. Using criminal justice risk assessment as a focal case, we conduct a synthetic review connecting three intertwined aspects of AI's role in judicial decision-making: the performance and fairness of AI tools, the strengths and biases of human judges, and the nature of AI+human interactions. Across the fields of computer science, economics, law, criminology and psychology, researchers have made significant progress in evaluating the predictive validity of automated risk assessment instruments, documenting biases in judicial decision-making, and, to a more limited extent, examining how judges use algorithmic recommendations. While the existing empirical evidence indicates that the impact of AI decision aid tools on pretrial and sentencing decisions is modest or inexistent, our review also reveals important gaps in the canvassed literatures. Further research is needed to evaluate the performance of AI risk assessment instruments, understand how judges navigate noisy decision making environments and how individual characteristics influence judges' responses to AI advice. We argue that AI vs Human comparisons have the potential to yield new insights into both algorithmic tools and human decision-makers and advocate greater interdisciplinary integration and cross-fertilization in future research.

</details>


### [171] [Serendipity by Design: Evaluating the Impact of Cross-domain Mappings on Human and LLM Creativity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19087)
*Qiawen Ella Liu,Marina Dubova,Henry Conklin,Takumi Harada,Thomas L. Griffiths*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究比较了人类与大型语言模型在创造力方面的差异，特别是测试了"跨领域映射"干预对两者创意生成的影响，发现人类从随机跨领域映射中获益，而LLMs整体创意水平更高但对该干预不敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究大型语言模型是否与人类具有相似的创造力机制，以及相同的创造力干预措施（特别是跨领域映射）是否对两者都有效，以理解人工智能与人类在创造性思维方面的异同。

Method: 让人类参与者和LLMs在两种提示条件下为10种日常产品生成新功能：1)跨领域映射（从随机分配的远程源领域如章鱼、仙人掌、GPS中提取属性进行类比）；2)用户需求（针对未满足的用户需求提出创新）。比较两种条件下的创意产出。

Result: 人类可靠地从随机跨领域映射中获益，而LLMs平均生成比人类更原创的想法，但对跨领域映射干预没有显著的统计效应。然而，在两个系统中，当灵感源与目标语义距离更远时，跨领域映射的影响都会增加。

Conclusion: 研究结果强调了远程关联在创意构思中的作用，以及人类和LLMs对相同创造力干预措施的系统性差异，揭示了人工智能与人类创造性思维机制的不同。

Abstract: Are large language models (LLMs) creative in the same way humans are, and can the same interventions increase creativity in both? We evaluate a promising but largely untested intervention for creativity: forcing creators to draw an analogy from a random, remote source domain (''cross-domain mapping''). Human participants and LLMs generated novel features for ten daily products (e.g., backpack, TV) under two prompts: (i) cross-domain mapping, which required translating a property from a randomly assigned source (e.g., octopus, cactus, GPS), and (ii) user-need, which required proposing innovations targeting unmet user needs. We show that humans reliably benefit from randomly assigned cross-domain mappings, while LLMs, on average, generate more original ideas than humans and do not show a statistically significant effect of cross-domain mappings. However, in both systems, the impact of cross-domain mapping increases when the inspiration source becomes more semantically distant from the target. Our results highlight both the role of remote association in creative ideation and systematic differences in how humans and LLMs respond to the same intervention for creativity.

</details>


### [172] [LuMamba: Latent Unified Mamba for Electrode Topology-Invariant and Efficient EEG Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19100)
*Danaé Broustail,Anna Tegon,Thorir Mar Ingolfsson,Yawei Li,Luca Benini*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LuMamba：结合拓扑不变编码与线性复杂度状态空间建模的自监督EEG基础模型，在计算效率和跨电极配置泛化方面表现优异


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EEG基础模型面临两大挑战：不同电极拓扑结构的差异性和Transformer架构的二次方序列计算复杂度。需要一种既能处理不同电极配置又能高效处理长序列的解决方案。

Method: 提出LuMamba框架，结合拓扑不变编码（使用LUNA的learned-query cross-attention机制实现通道统一）和线性复杂度状态空间建模（使用FEMBA的双向Mamba块）。首次系统研究LeJEPA（Latent-Euclidean Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture）在生物信号学习中的应用。在TUEG语料库超过21,000小时无标签EEG数据上进行预训练。

Result: 仅460万参数，在TUAB任务上达到80.99%平衡准确率，在阿尔茨海默症检测上达到0.97 AUPR（state-of-art）。相比现有最优模型，在相同序列长度下计算量减少377倍，在达到典型GPU内存限制前可处理12倍长的序列。预训练目标中，掩码重建与LeJEPA结合效果最佳。

Conclusion: LuMamba通过拓扑不变编码和线性复杂度建模，有效解决了EEG基础模型面临的拓扑差异和计算可扩展性问题，为生物信号学习提供了高效且泛化能力强的解决方案。

Abstract: Electroencephalography (EEG) enables non-invasive monitoring of brain activity across clinical and neurotechnology applications, yet building foundation models for EEG remains challenging due to \emph{differing electrode topologies} and \emph{computational scalability}, as Transformer architectures incur quadratic sequence complexity. As a joint solution, we propose \textbf{LuMamba} (\textbf{L}atent \textbf{U}nified \textbf{Mamba}), a self-supervised framework combining topology-invariant encodings with linear-complexity state-space modeling, using LUNA's learned-query cross-attention mechanism for channel unification~\cite{luna}, and FEMBA's bidirectional Mamba blocks for efficient temporal modeling~\cite{femba}. Within this architecture, we provide the first systematic investigation of the Latent-Euclidean Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture (LeJEPA) for biosignal learning. Pre-trained on over 21,000 hours of unlabeled EEG from the TUEG corpus, LuMamba is evaluated on five downstream tasks spanning abnormality detection, artifact recognition, and mental condition classification across electrode configurations ranging from 16 to 26 channels. In the pre-training objective, masked reconstruction alone yields structured but less generalizable representations, while LeJEPA alone produces diffuse embeddings; combining both objectives achieves the most robust performance. With only 4.6M parameters, LuMamba attains 80.99\% balanced accuracy on TUAB and achieves state-of-art performance on Alzheimer's detection (0.97 AUPR), while requiring \textbf{377$\times$ fewer FLOPS} than state-of-art models at equivalent sequence lengths and scaling to \textbf{12$\times$ longer sequences} before reaching typical GPU memory limits. Code is available at https://github.com/pulp-bio/biofoundation

</details>


### [173] [How Uncertainty Estimation Scales with Sampling in Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19118)
*Maksym Del,Markus Kängsepp,Marharyta Domnich,Ardi Tampuu,Lisa Yankovskaya,Meelis Kull,Mark Fishel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文研究了推理语言模型在扩展思维链推理中的不确定性估计问题，通过并行采样方法分析自我一致性和语言化置信度两种信号在不同领域任务中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不确定性估计对于部署推理语言模型至关重要，但在扩展思维链推理场景下仍缺乏深入理解。需要研究完全黑盒方法（并行采样）在语言化置信度和自我一致性方面的表现。

Method: 采用并行采样作为完全黑盒方法，分析语言化置信度和自我一致性两种不确定性信号。在三个推理模型和17个任务（涵盖数学、STEM和人文学科）上进行实验，研究这些信号的扩展特性。

Result: 自我一致性和语言化置信度在推理模型中都能扩展，但自我一致性初始区分度较低，在中等采样下落后于语言化置信度。主要不确定性增益来自信号组合：仅用两个样本，混合估计器平均提升AUROC达+12，即使扩展到更大采样预算也优于单独信号。效果具有领域依赖性：在数学领域（RLVR风格后训练的本土领域），推理模型获得更高的不确定性质量，表现出更强的互补性和更快的扩展性。

Conclusion: 不确定性估计在扩展思维链推理中具有重要价值，信号组合（语言化置信度+自我一致性）比单独使用任一信号更有效，且效果随领域变化。数学领域的不确定性质量最高，表明后训练对不确定性估计有领域特异性影响。

Abstract: Uncertainty estimation is critical for deploying reasoning language models, yet remains poorly understood under extended chain-of-thought reasoning. We study parallel sampling as a fully black-box approach using verbalized confidence and self-consistency. Across three reasoning models and 17 tasks spanning mathematics, STEM, and humanities, we characterize how these signals scale.
  Both self-consistency and verbalized confidence scale in reasoning models, but self-consistency exhibits lower initial discrimination and lags behind verbalized confidence under moderate sampling. Most uncertainty gains, however, arise from signal combination: with just two samples, a hybrid estimator improves AUROC by up to $+12$ on average and already outperforms either signal alone even when scaled to much larger budgets, after which returns diminish. These effects are domain-dependent: in mathematics, the native domain of RLVR-style post-training, reasoning models achieve higher uncertainty quality and exhibit both stronger complementarity and faster scaling than in STEM or humanities.

</details>


### [174] [Implicit Patterns in LLM-Based Binary Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19138)
*Qiang Li,XiangRui Zhang,Haining Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究首次大规模分析了LLM驱动的二进制漏洞分析中的推理模式，发现了四种主导的隐式模式，这些模式形成了稳定的结构化系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于LLM的二进制漏洞分析通常采用迭代多轮推理方式，但系统如何组织数百个推理步骤的探索过程仍不明确，主要受限于上下文窗口和隐式的token级行为。

Method: 对521个二进制文件进行大规模、trace级别的分析，共包含99,563个推理步骤，识别LLM推理中出现的结构化隐式模式。

Result: 识别出四种主导的隐式模式：早期剪枝、路径依赖锁定、针对性回溯和知识引导优先级排序。这些模式形成了具有不同时间角色和可测量特征的稳定结构化系统。

Conclusion: 该研究首次系统性地表征了LLM驱动的二进制分析，为构建更可靠的分析系统奠定了基础，揭示了LLM推理中隐式决策如何组织探索过程而非依赖显式控制流或预定义启发式。

Abstract: Binary vulnerability analysis is increasingly performed by LLM-based agents in an iterative, multi-pass manner, with the model as the core decision-maker. However, how such systems organize exploration over hundreds of reasoning steps remains poorly understood, due to limited context windows and implicit token-level behaviors. We present the first large-scale, trace-level study showing that multi-pass LLM reasoning gives rise to structured, token-level implicit patterns. Analyzing 521 binaries with 99,563 reasoning steps, we identify four dominant patterns: early pruning, path-dependent lock-in, targeted backtracking, and knowledge-guided prioritization that emerge implicitly from reasoning traces. These token-level implicit patterns serve as an abstraction of LLM reasoning: instead of explicit control-flow or predefined heuristics, exploration is organized through implicit decisions regulating path selection, commitment, and revision. Our analysis shows these patterns form a stable, structured system with distinct temporal roles and measurable characteristics. Our results provide the first systematic characterization of LLM-driven binary analysis and a foundation for more reliable analysis systems.

</details>


### [175] [D5P4: Partition Determinantal Point Process for Diversity in Parallel Discrete Diffusion Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19146)
*Jonathan Lys,Vincent Gripon,Bastien Pasdeloup,Axel Marmoret,Lukas Mauch,Fabien Cardinaux,Ghouthi Boukli Hacene*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了D5P4，一种基于行列式点过程的离散扩散模型束搜索解码框架，用于提升文本生成的批次内多样性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散扩散模型是文本生成中自回归模型的有前景替代方案，但其解码方法研究不足。标准的自回归解码方法（如束搜索）不直接适用于迭代去噪过程，现有扩散解码技术对批次内多样性的控制有限。

Method: 提出了一个通用的离散扩散束搜索框架，支持并行生成候选序列和模块化的束选择目标。具体实现D5P4将选择步骤建模为行列式点过程的MAP推断，利用可扩展的贪心求解器，保持多GPU兼容性，并允许在模型概率和目标多样性之间进行显式权衡。

Result: 在自由生成和问答任务上的实验表明，D5P4在保持竞争力的生成质量的同时，显著提升了多样性表现，优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: D5P4为离散扩散模型提供了一种有效的多样性控制解码方法，通过行列式点过程实现模型概率与多样性的显式权衡，在多样性和质量之间取得了良好平衡。

Abstract: Discrete diffusion models are promising alternatives to autoregressive approaches for text generation, yet their decoding methods remain under-studied. Standard decoding methods for autoregressive models, such as beam search, do not directly apply to iterative denoising, and existing diffusion decoding techniques provide limited control over in-batch diversity. To bridge this gap, we introduce a generalized beam-search framework for discrete diffusion that generates candidates in parallel and supports modular beam-selection objectives. As a diversity-focused instantiation, we propose D5P4, which formulates the selection step as MAP inference over a Determinantal Point Process. Leveraging a scalable greedy solver, D5P4 maintains multi-GPU compatibility and enables an explicit trade-off between model probability and target diversity with near-zero compute overhead. Experiments on free-form generation and question answering demonstrate that D5P4 improves diversity over strong baselines while maintaining competitive generation quality.

</details>


### [176] [cuGenOpt: A GPU-Accelerated General-Purpose Metaheuristic Framework for Combinatorial Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19163)
*Yuyang Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: cuGenOpt是一个GPU加速的通用元启发式框架，通过CUDA架构、统一编码抽象和JIT编译等技术，在通用性、性能和易用性三个维度上同时取得突破，显著优于传统MIP求解器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组合优化问题在物流、调度和资源分配中普遍存在，但现有方法在通用性、性能和易用性之间存在根本性权衡。需要一种能够同时解决这三个维度的创新方法。

Method: 采用三层架构：引擎层使用"一个块演化一个解"的CUDA架构，包含统一编码抽象、两级自适应算子选择和硬件感知资源管理；可扩展层提供用户自定义算子注册接口；易用性层通过JIT编译提供纯Python API和基于LLM的建模助手。

Result: 在三种GPU架构上的实验表明：相比通用MIP求解器有数量级优势；在n=150实例上与专用求解器竞争；TSP-442在30秒内达到4.73%间隙；12种问题类型在5种编码变体上达到最优；框架优化将pcb442间隙从36%降至4.73%，VRPTW吞吐量提升75-81%。

Conclusion: cuGenOpt成功解决了组合优化中通用性、性能和易用性的权衡问题，通过GPU加速和智能框架设计为大规模组合优化问题提供了高效解决方案，具有广泛的应用前景。

Abstract: Combinatorial optimization problems arise in logistics, scheduling, and resource allocation, yet existing approaches face a fundamental trade-off among generality, performance, and usability. We present cuGenOpt, a GPU-accelerated general-purpose metaheuristic framework that addresses all three dimensions simultaneously.
  At the engine level, cuGenOpt adopts a "one block evolves one solution" CUDA architecture with a unified encoding abstraction (permutation, binary, integer), a two-level adaptive operator selection mechanism, and hardware-aware resource management. At the extensibility level, a user-defined operator registration interface allows domain experts to inject problem-specific CUDA search operators. At the usability level, a JIT compilation pipeline exposes the framework as a pure-Python API, and an LLM-based modeling assistant converts natural-language problem descriptions into executable solver code.
  Experiments across five thematic suites on three GPU architectures (T4, V100, A800) show that cuGenOpt outperforms general MIP solvers by orders of magnitude, achieves competitive quality against specialized solvers on instances up to n=150, and attains 4.73% gap on TSP-442 within 30s. Twelve problem types spanning five encoding variants are solved to optimality. Framework-level optimizations cumulatively reduce pcb442 gap from 36% to 4.73% and boost VRPTW throughput by 75-81%.
  Code: https://github.com/L-yang-yang/cugenopt

</details>


### [177] [Box Maze: A Process-Control Architecture for Reliable LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19182)
*Zou Qiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Box Maze框架，通过三层认知控制架构（记忆锚定、结构化推理、边界执行）来增强LLM推理过程完整性，在对抗条件下将边界失败率从约40%降至1%以下。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM虽然具备强大生成能力，但在对抗提示下容易产生幻觉和不可靠推理。现有的安全方法（如RLHF和输出过滤）主要在行为层面操作，缺乏确保推理过程完整性的显式架构机制。

Method: 提出Box Maze框架，将LLM推理分解为三个显式层次：1) 记忆锚定层，确保推理基于可靠记忆；2) 结构化推理层，强制执行逻辑结构；3) 边界执行层，维护安全边界。通过渐进边界侵蚀场景在多个异构LLM系统（DeepSeek-V3、Doubao、Qwen）上进行模拟评估。

Result: 在50个对抗场景的初步评估中，显式认知控制层提高了边界维护的一致性。架构约束将边界失败率从基线RLHF的约40%降至对抗条件下低于1%。

Conclusion: 虽然当前验证基于模拟，但初步结果表明过程级控制为提高大语言模型推理可靠性提供了一个有前景的方向。Box Maze框架通过显式架构机制增强了推理过程的完整性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) demonstrate strong generative capabilities but remain vulnerable to hallucination and unreliable reasoning under adversarial prompting. Existing safety approaches -- such as reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF) and output filtering -- primarily operate at the behavioral level and may lack explicit architectural mechanisms for enforcing reasoning process integrity.
  This paper proposes the Box Maze framework, a conceptual process-control architecture that decomposes LLM reasoning into three explicit layers: memory grounding, structured inference, and boundary enforcement. We introduce preliminary simulation-based evaluation involving progressive boundary erosion scenarios across multiple heterogeneous LLM systems (DeepSeek-V3, Doubao, Qwen). Results from n=50 adversarial scenarios suggest that explicit cognitive control layers may improve consistency in boundary maintenance, with architectural constraints reducing boundary failure rates from approximately 40% (baseline RLHF) to below 1% under adversarial conditions.
  While current validation is simulation-based, these preliminary results indicate that process-level control may offer a promising direction for improving reliability in large language model reasoning.

</details>


### [178] [OS-Themis: A Scalable Critic Framework for Generalist GUI Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19191)
*Zehao Li,Zhenyu Wu,Yibo Zhao,Bowen Yang,Jingjing Xie,Zhaoyang Liu,Zhoumianze Liu,Kaiming Jin,Jianze Liang,Zonglin Li,Feng Wu,Bowen Zhou,Zun Wang,Zichen Ding*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OS-Themis是一个可扩展且准确的多智能体批评框架，通过将轨迹分解为可验证的里程碑来改进GUI智能体的强化学习奖励函数，在AndroidWorld实验中带来10.3%的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习在随机环境中提升GUI智能体鲁棒性具有潜力，但训练对奖励函数质量高度敏感。现有奖励方法难以同时实现可扩展性和高性能。

Method: 提出OS-Themis多智能体批评框架：1) 将轨迹分解为可验证的里程碑，分离关键决策证据；2) 采用审查机制严格审计证据链后做出最终裁决。还引入了OmniGUIRewardBench跨平台GUI结果奖励基准。

Result: 在AndroidWorld上的实验表明：1) OS-Themis支持在线RL训练时带来10.3%的性能提升；2) 在自训练循环中用于轨迹验证和过滤时带来6.9%的增益。所有评估模型在OS-Themis下都达到最佳性能。

Conclusion: OS-Themis通过分解轨迹和严格证据审计，有效解决了GUI智能体强化学习中奖励函数的可扩展性和准确性问题，具有推动智能体进化的潜力。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning (RL) has the potential to improve the robustness of GUI agents in stochastic environments, yet training is highly sensitive to the quality of the reward function. Existing reward approaches struggle to achieve both scalability and performance. To address this, we propose OS-Themis, a scalable and accurate multi-agent critic framework. Unlike a single judge, OS-Themis decomposes trajectories into verifiable milestones to isolate critical evidence for decision making and employs a review mechanism to strictly audit the evidence chain before making the final verdict. To facilitate evaluation, we further introduce OmniGUIRewardBench (OGRBench), a holistic cross-platform benchmark for GUI outcome rewards, where all evaluated models achieve their best performance under OS-Themis. Extensive experiments on AndroidWorld show that OS-Themis yields a 10.3% improvement when used to support online RL training, and a 6.9% gain when used for trajectory validation and filtering in the self-training loop, highlighting its potential to drive agent evolution.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [179] [Using Laplace Transform To Optimize the Hallucination of Generation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18022)
*Cheng Kang,Xinye Chen,Daniel Novak,Xujing Yao*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文从控制理论视角将生成模型形式化为随机动力系统，通过拉普拉斯变换分析从根本上优化生成模型的幻觉问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索避免生成模型产生自信错误（幻觉）的可行性，从控制理论角度分析生成模型系统

Method: 将生成模型形式化为随机动力系统，利用控制理论分析系统函数和系统响应，采用拉普拉斯变换分析，从宏观角度模拟源响应

Result: 发现训练进度与对应系统响应一致，为开发更好的优化组件提供了有用方法，通过拉普拉斯变换分析从根本上优化了幻觉问题

Conclusion: 控制理论为分析生成模型幻觉问题提供了新视角，拉普拉斯变换分析是优化幻觉问题的有效方法，模拟源响应是解决幻觉问题的虚拟途径

Abstract: To explore the feasibility of avoiding the confident error (or hallucination) of generation models (GMs), we formalise the system of GMs as a class of stochastic dynamical systems through the lens of control theory. Numerous factors can be attributed to the hallucination of the learning process of GMs, utilising knowledge of control theory allows us to analyse their system functions and system responses. Due to the high complexity of GMs when using various optimization methods, we cannot figure out their solution of Laplace transform, but from a macroscopic perspective, simulating the source response provides a virtual way to address the hallucination of GMs. We also find that the training progress is consistent with the corresponding system response, which offers us a useful way to develop a better optimization component. Finally, the hallucination problem of GMs is fundamentally optimized by using Laplace transform analysis.

</details>


### [180] [A Hybrid Decomposition Approach for Stochastic Unit Commitment with Combined-Cycle Generators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18177)
*Rosemary Barrass,Harsha Nagarajan,Mathieu Tanneau,Russell Bent,Pascal Van Hentenryck*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种混合Benders分解和Dantzig-Wolfe分解算法，用于解决包含联合循环发电机（CCs）的随机机组组合问题，相比传统方法显著提升求解速度


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 美国电网正经历重大变革，可再生能源、电动汽车和数据中心的发展增加了对快速响应发电机的需求。联合循环发电机（CCs）因其高效率而被广泛建设，但其在机组组合问题中引入了大量二元约束和变量，增加了求解难度。

Method: 提出混合Benders分解（BD）和Dantzig-Wolfe分解（DW）算法：利用BD分解线性约束，利用DW分解整数CC约束。在包含CC模式数据的935台发电机FERC测试数据集上进行验证。

Result: 相比传统BD算法在所有案例中都显示出显著加速；在25个或更多场景的案例中，比BD和Gurobi分支定界求解器具有更好的收敛速度；证明该算法是解决随机机组组合问题的可扩展方法。

Conclusion: 提出的混合BD-DW分解算法能有效处理CCs带来的复杂性，为大规模随机机组组合问题提供了高效、可扩展的求解方案。

Abstract: The U.S. power grid is undergoing a major paradigm shift with the increased development of renewable generators, electric vehicles, and data centers. In response to this growing need, the U.S. has ramped up the construction of combined-cycle generators (CCs). CCs are fast-ramping generators that utilize variable configurations of combustion turbines (CTs) and steam turbines (STs) to achieve much higher efficiency than traditional CTs alone. For schedule optimization, this requires the addition of a large number of binary constraints and variables in Unit Commitment (UC) problem formulations. This paper presents a novel hybrid Benders' (BD) and Dantzig-Wolfe (DW) decomposition algorithm for stochastic UC problems with CCs. The algorithm exploits the separability of the linear constraints in UC through BD and the integer CC constraints through DW. Results are presented for the 935-generator FERC test data set, modified to include mode data for CCs. The algorithm demonstrates a significant speed-up over traditional BD across all cases. It also demonstrates better convergence rates on cases with 25 or more scenarios than both BD and Gurobi's branch-and-bound solver. These cases show that the proposed algorithm is a scalable approach for solving stochastic UC.

</details>


### [181] [Solving Sparsity Constrained PCA, Regression, and QCQP via the Spartrahedron](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18215)
*Diego Cifuentes,Zhuorui Li*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于新型凸锥"spartrahedron"的稀疏二次约束二次规划凸松弛框架，能精确刻画矩阵级稀疏性，产生紧致的半定规划松弛，为稀疏PCA、稀疏回归等问题提供全局最优性证书。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏性是统计学、信号处理和数据科学中的基本建模原则，但带稀疏约束的优化问题极其困难。现有方法在处理稀疏二次约束二次规划这类包含稀疏回归、稀疏主成分分析等关键问题时面临挑战，需要新的凸松弛框架来有效解决这些难题。

Method: 引入新型凸锥"spartrahedron"，该锥能在矩阵层面精确刻画稀疏性。基于此构建稀疏二次约束二次规划的凸松弛框架，产生半定规划松弛。当解为秩一时，松弛是紧致的，从而提供全局最优性的简单证书。

Result: 建立了理论保证，包括稀疏PCA和稀疏岭回归的近似界和精确性区域，以及扰动下的一般稳定性结果。在稀疏PCA、稀疏回归、RIP常数估计和稀疏典型相关分析上的数值实验证明了方法的实际有效性。

Conclusion: 提出的基于spartrahedron的凸松弛框架为稀疏二次约束二次规划提供了有效的解决方案，具有理论保证和实际应用价值，能处理包括稀疏PCA、稀疏回归在内的多种重要稀疏优化问题。

Abstract: Sparsity is a fundamental modeling principle in statistics, signal processing, and data science. However, optimization with sparsity constraints is notoriously difficult. We introduce a new convex relaxation framework for {sparse quadratically constrained quadratic programs} (QCQPs), a class that subsumes sparse regression, sparse principal component analysis (PCA), and related problems. Our approach is based on a novel convex cone, the spartrahedron, which exactly characterizes sparsity at the matrix level. This leads to a semidefinite programming (SDP) relaxation that is tight whenever its solution is rank-one, providing a simple certificate of global optimality. We establish theoretical guarantees, including approximation bounds and exactness regions for sparse PCA and sparse ridge regression, as well as a general stability result under perturbations. Numerical experiments on sparse PCA, sparse regression, RIP constant estimation, and sparse canonical correlation analysis (CCA) demonstrate the practical success of our methods.

</details>


### [182] [Forward-Backward Dynamic Programming for LQG Dynamic Games with Partial and Asymmetric Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18304)
*Yuxiang Guan,Iman Shames,Tyler Summers*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出并研究了具有部分和不对称信息的两人零和随机动态博弈，通过线性二次高斯模型和有限内部状态维度策略解决信念表示和心智理论问题，开发了有限时域和无限时域两种算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信息不对称在随机动态博弈中引入了根本性挑战，涉及信念表示和心智理论问题，需要避免无限高阶信念回归并获得可计算的结果。

Method: 采用线性二次高斯模型，考虑有限内部状态维度的策略，提出了有限时域的迭代前向后向算法和无限时域的平均成本值迭代算法。

Result: 开发了计算信念状态、均衡策略和价值函数的算法，提供了开源实现，并通过数值实验验证了算法的有效性。

Conclusion: 提出的方法能够有效处理部分和不对称信息下的随机动态博弈，避免了无限高阶信念问题，提供了可计算且有效的解决方案。

Abstract: We formulate and study a class of two-player zero-sum stochastic dynamic games with partial and asymmetric information. Information asymmetry introduces fundamental challenges involving \emph{belief representation} and \emph{theory of mind} issues, where agents must impute belief states and estimates of other agents to inform their own strategy. To avoid an infinite regress of higher-order beliefs amongst agents and obtain computationally implementable results, we focus on a linear quadratic Gaussian (LQG) model and consider strategies with limited internal state dimension. We present a novel iterative forward-backward algorithm to jointly compute belief states and equilibrium strategies and value functions for a finite-horizon problem. We also present a value iteration-like algorithm to jointly compute stationary belief states and equilibrium strategies for an average-cost infinite-horizon problem. An open-source implementation of the algorithms is provided, and we demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed algorithms in numerical experiments.

</details>


### [183] [Adversarial Robustness for Matrix Control Barrier Functions in Sampled-Data Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18307)
*James Usevitch*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出基于矩阵控制屏障函数的多智能体采样数据系统集合不变性保证理论，包括异构控制仿射智能体零阶保持控制、对抗性智能体处理以及高相对度系统扩展


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决多智能体采样数据系统中集合不变性保证的理论问题，特别是在存在对抗性智能体和系统高相对度情况下的安全控制挑战

Method: 使用矩阵控制屏障函数方法，为异构控制仿射智能体建立零阶保持控制输入计算条件，设计对抗性智能体处理机制，并扩展到高相对度系统

Result: 建立了保证多智能体集合不变性的充分条件，提出了对抗性智能体存在时的安全控制方法，并成功将理论扩展到高相对度系统

Conclusion: 该理论框架为多智能体采样数据系统提供了全面的集合不变性保证，能够处理异构性、对抗性行为和高相对度等复杂情况，增强了系统的安全性和鲁棒性

Abstract: This paper presents novel theoretical results to guarantee multi-agent set invariance using Matrix Control Barrier Functions in sampled-data systems. More specifically, the paper presents conditions under which heterogeneous control-affine agents applying zero-order-hold control inputs can compute control inputs to render safe sets defined by matrix inequalities forward invariant. It then introduces methods to guarantee set invariance while accounting for the presence of adversarial agents seeking to drive the system state to unsafe sets. Finally, the paper presents theoretical extensions of these set invariance results to systems having high relative degree with respect to the matrix-valued safe set function.

</details>


### [184] [Stabilization of highly nonlinear hybrid stochastic differential delay equations by periodically intermittent feedback controls based on discrete-time observations with asynchronous switching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18367)
*Guangqiang Lan,Fansai Meng*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究高度非线性混合随机时滞微分方程的矩指数镇定问题，设计基于离散时间状态观测的异步切换周期性间歇控制器，获得观测周期上界和控制宽度下界，证明系统的p阶矩有限性和有界性，建立指数稳定性及收敛速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高度非线性混合随机时滞微分方程在实际工程系统中广泛存在，但这类系统的稳定性分析面临挑战。需要设计有效的控制器来实现系统的指数镇定，特别是考虑到实际应用中只能进行离散时间状态观测和间歇控制的情况。

Method: 设计基于离散时间状态观测的异步切换周期性间歇控制器。首先在广义Khasminskii型条件下建立解p阶矩的有限性和有界性，然后给出控制函数、漂移系数和扩散系数的合理条件，最后证明受控系统的指数稳定性及收敛速率。

Result: 获得了观测周期的上界和控制宽度的下界，证明了系统在设计的控制器下能够实现矩指数稳定，并给出了收敛速率的表达式。通过示例表明控制区间的比例与收敛速率呈正相关关系。

Conclusion: 提出的基于离散时间状态观测的异步切换周期性间歇控制器能够有效镇定高度非线性混合随机时滞微分方程。理论分析和数值示例均验证了控制方法的有效性，且控制区间比例越大，系统收敛越快。

Abstract: In this paper, we will investigate the moment exponential stabilization of highly nonlinear hybrid stochastic differential delay equations. A periodically intermittent controller based on discrete time state observations with asynchronous switching is designed. The upper bound of observation period as well as the lower bound of the control width are all obtained. Firstly, the finiteness and boundedness of the $p$-th moment of the solution are established under a generalized Khasminskii-type condition. Then reasonable conditions of control function, drift and diffusion coefficients are presented. Then exponential stability as well as the convergence rate of controlled system are proved. Finally, an example is presented to interpret the conclusion, which also indicates that the proportion of control interval has positive relation to the convergence rate.

</details>


### [185] [Fundamental Limits for Sensor-Based Control via the Gibbs Variational Principle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18454)
*Vincent Pacelli,Evangelos A. Theodorou*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种基于吉布斯变分原理的自洽下界方法，用于计算部分观测下因果反馈控制器的最小期望成本，解决了现有信息论方法在反馈最有价值时性能下降的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有信息论方法通过评估传感器相对于非受控系统的性能来高估传感器必须提供的信息，导致在反馈最有价值时边界性能下降。需要一种通用工具来计算反馈控制器的性能基本极限，用于算法基准测试、传感器选择和任务可行性认证。

Method: 通过将吉布斯变分原理应用于状态和观测的联合路径测度，推导出部分观测下任何因果反馈控制器的最小期望成本下界。该方法适用于非线性、非完整和混合动力学系统，具有无界成本，并允许自洽细化：好的控制器集中状态，限制传感器可提取信息，从而收紧边界。通过二分法计算固定点方程的唯一解，并在自由能最小化可证明凸的条件下提供可认证正确的数值边界。

Result: 在非线性Dubins汽车跟踪问题上，自洽边界在传感器噪声水平范围内捕获了大部分最优成本，而开环变体在低噪声下无效。该方法能够准确评估反馈控制器的性能极限。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了一种计算部分观测下反馈控制器性能基本极限的通用工具，解决了现有信息论方法的局限性，通过自洽细化机制在反馈最有价值时提供更紧的边界，为控制系统设计和分析提供了重要理论工具。

Abstract: Fundamental limits on the performance of feedback controllers are essential for benchmarking algorithms, guiding sensor selection, and certifying task feasibility -- yet few general-purpose tools exist for computing them. Existing information-theoretic approaches overestimate the information a sensor must provide by evaluating it against the uncontrolled system, producing bounds that degrade precisely when feedback is most valuable. We derive a lower bound on the minimum expected cost of any causal feedback controller under partial observations by applying the Gibbs variational principle to the joint path measure over states and observations. The bound applies to nonlinear, nonholonomic, and hybrid dynamics with unbounded costs and admits a self-consistent refinement: any good controller concentrates the state, which limits the information the sensor can extract, which tightens the bound. The resulting fixed-point equation has a unique solution computable by bisection, and we provide conditions under which the free energy minimization is provably convex, yielding a certifiably correct numerical bound. On a nonlinear Dubins car tracking problem, the self-consistent bound captures most of the optimal cost across sensor noise levels, while the open-loop variant is vacuous at low noise.

</details>


### [186] [Axis-Aligned Relaxations for Mixed-Integer Nonlinear Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18458)
*Haisheng Zhu,Taotao He,Mohit Tawarmalani*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出基于计算几何的混合整数非线性规划松弛框架，通过凸化策略选择点集构建多面体松弛，迭代逼近函数图的同时凸包，在多项式和MINLPLib实例上显著提升对偶界质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有混合整数非线性规划松弛方法（如因子化松弛）存在局限性，需要更紧致的松弛来提升求解效率。计算几何方法为构建高质量松弛提供了新途径，但尚未系统应用于MINLP领域。

Method: 基于计算几何构建多面体松弛框架：1) 提出函数乘积的新显式不等式类，改进标准因子化和复合松弛方案；2) 证明轴对齐区域上多线性函数的同时凸包完全由角点值确定；3) 集成体素化和QuickHull等计算几何工具，高效近似可行域和函数图。

Result: 在随机生成多项式优化问题和MINLPLib的619个实例上验证：多项式实例中，相比标准方法在50%实例上额外缩小20-25%最优性间隙；相比增强因子化编程基线和Gurobi根节点界，在约30% MINLPLib实例上获得更优对偶界，约10%实例间隙减少超过50%。

Conclusion: 计算几何方法为MINLP松弛提供了有效框架，通过系统构建多面体近似显著提升松弛质量，为混合整数非线性规划求解器开发提供了新方向。

Abstract: We present a novel relaxation framework for general mixed-integer nonlinear programming (MINLP) grounded in computational geometry. Our approach constructs polyhedral relaxations by convexifying finite sets of strategically chosen points, iteratively refining the approximation to converge toward the simultaneous convex hull of factorable function graphs. The framework is underpinned by three key contributions: (i) a new class of explicit inequalities for products of functions that strictly improve upon standard factorable and composite relaxation schemes; (ii) a proof establishing that the simultaneous convex hull of multilinear functions over axis-aligned regions is fully determined by their values at corner points, thereby generalizing existing results from hypercubes to arbitrary axis-aligned domains; and (iii) the integration of computational geometry tools, specifically voxelization and QuickHull, to efficiently approximate feasible regions and function graphs. We implement this framework and evaluate it on randomly generated polynomial optimization problems and a suite of 619 instances from \texttt{MINLPLib}. Numerical results demonstrate significant improvements over state-of-the-art benchmarks: on polynomial instances, our relaxation closes an additional 20--25\% of the optimality gap relative to standard methods on half the instances. Furthermore, compared against an enhanced factorable programming baseline and Gurobi's root-node bounds, our approach yields superior dual bounds on approximately 30\% of \texttt{MINLPLib} instances, with roughly 10\% of cases exhibiting a gap reduction exceeding 50\%.

</details>


### [187] [Modeling Adversarial Wildfires for Power Grid Disruption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18473)
*Matthew Brun,Xu Andy Sun,Jean-Paul Watson*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出混合整数锥规划方法模拟对抗性野火对电力基础设施的攻击，基于Rothermel火蔓延模型设计风助火蔓延集，通过凸松弛技术求解最小停电时间和最大负荷削减，用于构建安全约束最优潮流模型提升电网抗野火韧性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电力基础设施面临日益严重的野火破坏风险，传统野火模型无法有效描述最坏情况或高影响火灾行为，需要开发能够模拟对抗性野火攻击的方法来评估和提升电网韧性。

Method: 提出混合整数锥规划方法，基于Rothermel火蔓延模型设计风助火蔓延集，开发新的欧几里得球内积凸松弛技术，利用加州Park、Eaton和Palisades火灾案例测试，计算最小停电时间和最大负荷削减。

Result: 成功识别多元件故障的最小停电时间和由现实野火引起的元件序列停电的最大负荷削减，利用最小停电时间值筛选故障并构建安全约束最优潮流模型，提升电网对野火的运行韧性。

Conclusion: 该方法能够有效模拟对抗性野火对电力基础设施的攻击，为电网运营商提供评估和提升抗野火韧性的工具，通过安全约束最优潮流模型促进电网在野火威胁下的运行韧性。

Abstract: Electric power infrastructure faces increasing risk of damage and disruption due to wildfire. Operators of power grids in wildfire-prone regions must consider the potential impacts of unpredictable fires. However, traditional wildfire models do not effectively describe worst-case, or even high-impact, fire behavior. To address this issue, we propose a mixed-integer conic program to characterize an adversarial wildfire that targets infrastructure while respecting realistic fire spread dynamics. We design a wind-assisted fire spread set based on the Rothermel fire spread model and propose principled convex relaxations of this set, including a new relaxation of the inner product over Euclidean balls. We present test cases derived from the recent Park, Eaton, and Palisades fires in California and solve models to identify the minimum time-to-outage of multiple-element contingencies and the maximum load shed associated with a sequence of element outages caused by a realistic wildfire. We use the minimum time-to-outage values to screen contingencies and construct security-constrained optimal power flow models that promote operational resilience against wildfire.

</details>


### [188] [Computationally Efficient Density-Driven Optimal Control via Analytical KKT Reduction and Contractive MPC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18503)
*Julian Martinez,Kooktae Lee*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种将多智能体空间分布控制的大规模KKT系统转化为紧凑二次规划的方法，实现线性计算复杂度，显著降低在线计算负担


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有密度驱动最优控制（D2OC）框架在实现预测控制时需要求解大规模KKT系统，其计算复杂度随预测时域呈立方增长，限制了大规模多智能体系统的长时域预测控制应用

Method: 提出解析结构约简方法，将T时域KKT系统转化为紧凑二次规划（QP），实现O(T)线性可扩展性；引入收缩李雅普诺夫约束，证明闭环系统对参考传播漂移的输入到状态稳定性（ISS）

Result: 方法显著降低了在线计算负担，相比传统O(T³)方法实现线性可扩展；数值仿真验证了该方法能促进快速密度覆盖，实现大规模多智能体群的长时域预测控制

Conclusion: 提出的结构约简方法有效解决了多智能体空间分布控制的计算瓶颈，通过线性可扩展的QP公式和严格的稳定性保证，为大规模智能体群的实时协调控制提供了可行方案

Abstract: Efficient coordination for collective spatial distribution is a fundamental challenge in multi-agent systems. Prior research on Density-Driven Optimal Control (D2OC) established a framework to match agent trajectories to a desired spatial distribution. However, implementing this as a predictive controller requires solving a large-scale Karush-Kuhn-Tucker (KKT) system, whose computational complexity grows cubically with the prediction horizon. To resolve this, we propose an analytical structural reduction that transforms the T-horizon KKT system into a condensed quadratic program (QP). This formulation achieves O(T) linear scalability, significantly reducing the online computational burden compared to conventional O(T^3) approaches. Furthermore, to ensure rigorous convergence in dynamic environments, we incorporate a contractive Lyapunov constraint and prove the Input-to-State Stability (ISS) of the closed-loop system against reference propagation drift. Numerical simulations verify that the proposed method facilitates rapid density coverage with substantial computational speed-up, enabling long-horizon predictive control for large-scale multi-agent swarms.

</details>


### [189] [Learning Decision-Sufficient Representations for Linear Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18551)
*Yuhan Ye,Saurabh Amin,Asuman Ozdauglar*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究线性规划中决策充分数据集的构建，证明了相关计算问题的NP困难性，提出了多项式时间的近似算法，并在数据驱动场景下获得了分布无关的PAC保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bennouna等人的工作虽然提供了决策充分数据集(SDDs)的几何刻画，但其构建最小尺寸SDDs的算法需要求解混合整数规划，计算复杂度高。本文旨在解决这一计算困难问题，并探索在数据驱动场景下的实用算法。

Method: 1) 证明计算决策相关维度d*是NP困难的，判断数据集是否全局充分是coNP困难的；2) 引入逐点充分性这一松弛概念；3) 在非退化条件下，提出多项式时间的割平面算法构建逐点充分决策数据集；4) 在i.i.d.成本数据驱动场景下，提出累积算法聚合决策相关方向；5) 将决策充分表示应用于上下文线性优化。

Result: 1) 建立了相关计算问题的硬度结果；2) 获得了分布无关的PAC保证：在训练样本上，新样本的逐点充分性失败概率最多为Õ(d*/n)，且该速率在忽略对数因子下是紧的；3) 在上下文线性优化中，获得了压缩预测器的泛化界，其尺度为Õ(√(d*/n))而非Õ(√(d/n))，其中d是环境成本维度。

Conclusion: 本文解决了线性规划中决策充分数据集构建的计算困难问题，通过引入逐点充分性松弛和多项式时间算法，在保持理论保证的同时实现了实用化。在数据驱动场景下，所提方法能够有效压缩数据集并保证泛化性能，为实际应用提供了理论基础和算法支持。

Abstract: We study how to construct compressed datasets that suffice to recover optimal decisions in linear programs with an unknown cost vector $c$ lying in a prior set $\mathcal{C}$. Recent work by Bennouna et al. provides an exact geometric characterization of sufficient decision datasets (SDDs) via an intrinsic decision-relevant dimension $d^\star$. However, their algorithm for constructing minimum-size SDDs requires solving mixed-integer programs. In this paper, we establish hardness results showing that computing $d^\star$ is NP-hard and deciding whether a dataset is globally sufficient is coNP-hard, thereby resolving a recent open problem posed by Bennouna et al. To address this worst-case intractability, we introduce pointwise sufficiency, a relaxation that requires sufficiency for an individual cost vector. Under nondegeneracy, we provide a polynomial-time cutting-plane algorithm for constructing pointwise-sufficient decision datasets. In a data-driven regime with i.i.d.\ costs, we further propose a cumulative algorithm that aggregates decision-relevant directions across samples, yielding a stable compression scheme of size at most $d^\star$. This leads to a distribution-free PAC guarantee: with high probability over the training sample, the pointwise sufficiency failure probability on a fresh draw is at most $\tilde{O}(d^\star/n)$, and this rate is tight up to logarithmic factors. Finally, we apply decision-sufficient representations to contextual linear optimization, obtaining compressed predictors with generalization bounds scaling as $\tilde{O}(\sqrt{d^\star/n})$ rather than $\tilde{O}(\sqrt{d/n})$, where $d$ is the ambient cost dimension.

</details>


### [190] [Robust mean-field games under entropy-based uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18628)
*François Delarue,Pierre Lavigne*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出熵惩罚鲁棒平均场博弈问题，其中代表性智能体与自然对抗，通过min-max随机控制建模，建立均衡存在性并扩展单调性条件保证唯一性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究在概率测度受到自然扭曲情况下的平均场博弈问题，其中智能体需要在不确定性环境下做出最优决策，自然以熵成本扭曲参考概率测度

Method: 使用min-max随机控制框架建模智能体与自然的对抗，通过Schauder不动点定理证明平均场博弈均衡存在性，引入联合平坦反单调性和位移单调性条件确保唯一性

Result: 建立了熵惩罚鲁棒平均场博弈均衡的存在性，在扩展的单调性条件下证明了唯一性，并展示了该平均场极限能为两类N-玩家博弈提供ε-纳什均衡

Conclusion: 成功构建了熵惩罚鲁棒平均场博弈理论框架，扩展了经典Lasry-Lions单调性条件，为不确定性环境下的多智能体决策提供了新的分析工具

Abstract: In this article, we introduce a new class of entropy-penalized robust mean field game problems in which the representative agent is opposed to Nature. The agent's objective is formulated as a min-max stochastic control problem, in which Nature distorts the reference probability measure at an entropic cost. As a consequence, the distribution of the continuum of agents represented by the player is given by the effective measure induced by Nature. Existence of a mean-field game equilibrium is established via a Schauder fixed point argument. To ensure uniqueness, we introduce a joint flat anti-monotonicity and displacement monotonicity condition, extending the classical Lasry-Lions monotonicity framework. Finally, we present two classes of N -player games for which the mean-field game limit yields $ε$-Nash equilibria.

</details>


### [191] [Robust Discrete Pricing Optimization via Multiple-Choice Knapsack Reductions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18653)
*Zi Yuan Eric Shao*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 论文研究了在利润率和公平性约束下，从有限价格菜单中选择产品价格的离散投资组合定价问题，采用预算鲁棒优化处理需求不确定性，通过转化为多选择背包问题获得精确解方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在零售和投资组合定价中，需要在有限价格菜单中选择产品价格，同时面临需求不确定性和利润、公平性约束。传统方法难以在计算可处理性和鲁棒性之间取得平衡，需要开发既能控制保守程度又能保持计算效率的定价方法。

Method: 将离散投资组合定价问题转化为多选择背包问题（MCKP），利用LP松弛的结构特性（上包络过滤和分段贪婪填充）获得固定参数子问题的精确LP松弛解。通过分析整数性间隙，证明在标准有界性和线性增长假设下，相对间隙以O(1/n)衰减。

Result: 整数性间隙有界于单项目包络跳跃，相对间隙在标准假设下以O(1/n)衰减。数值实验表明，在测试实例中，鲁棒利润率保护可以实现小于1%的名义收入损失。

Conclusion: 该方法在计算可处理性和鲁棒性之间取得了良好平衡，通过预算鲁棒优化控制保守程度，利用MCKP结构特性获得高效算法，为离散投资组合定价提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: We study a discrete portfolio pricing problem that selects one price per product from a finite menu under margin and fairness constraints. To account for demand uncertainty, we incorporate a budgeted robust formulation that controls conservatism while remaining computationally tractable. By reducing the problem to a Multiple-Choice Knapsack Problem (MCKP), we identify structural properties of the LP relaxation, in particular upper-hull filtering and greedy filling over hull segments, that yield an exact solution method for the LP relaxation of the fixed-parameter subproblems. For the resulting fixed-parameter subproblems, we show that the integrality gap is bounded additively by a single-item hull jump, and that the corresponding relative gap decays as O(1/n) under standard boundedness and linear-growth assumptions. Numerical experiments on synthetic portfolios and a stylized retail case study with economically calibrated parameters are consistent with these bounds and indicate that robust margin protection can be achieved with less than 1 percent nominal revenue loss on the instances tested.

</details>


### [192] [Stabilization of Integral Difference Equations by solving a Corona problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18703)
*Adam Braun,Jean Auriol,Lucas Brivadis*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种针对具有标量控制输入和点状/分布时滞的积分差分方程系统的稳定状态反馈控制律，通过积分算子实现，基于Corona问题的卷积方程求解控制器核函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有IDE（积分差分方程）稳定化方法存在局限性，需要扩展至更一般的设置，允许任意数量的点状时滞影响状态和输入，且无需可公度性假设。

Method: 通过积分算子作用于状态和输入历史来构造控制器；将控制器核函数表征为Corona问题产生的卷积方程的解；在谱可稳定条件下确保解的存在性；采用最小二乘程序进行数值求解。

Result: 建立了闭环稳定性，证明了在适当谱可稳定条件下控制器核函数解的存在性，并提供了数值求解方法，扩展了现有IDE稳定化结果。

Conclusion: 该方法成功扩展了IDE稳定化框架，能够处理具有任意数量点状和分布时滞的更一般系统，无需可公度性假设，为时滞系统的控制提供了更通用的解决方案。

Abstract: This paper proposes a stabilizing state-feedback control law for vector-valued state systems with a scalar control input, governed by a general class of integral difference equations that incorporate both pointwise and distributed input delays. The proposed controller is expressed through integral operators acting on the state and input histories over a finite time horizon. Closed-loop stability is established by characterizing the controller kernels as solutions to a convolution equation arising from a Corona problem. The existence of such solutions is ensured under a suitable spectral stabilizability condition, and a least-square procedure is implemented to find them numerically. The approach extends existing IDE stabilization results to more general settings, allowing for arbitrary numbers of pointwise delays affecting both the state and input, without requiring commensurability assumptions.

</details>


### [193] [A mathematical framework for time-delay reservoir computing analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18706)
*Anh-Tuan Clabaut,Jean Auriol,Islam Boussaada,Guilherme Mazanti*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文为时延储备池计算建立了控制理论框架，通过泛函范数形式化定义分离性和渐近记忆特性，并将其与时延系统的增量输入状态稳定性联系起来，为线性储备池提供了基于傅里叶分析的分离距离下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 储备池计算作为一种低复杂度数据处理方法已发展二十年，但其核心特性（分离性、鲁棒性、渐近记忆）仍缺乏严格的数学基础。本文旨在填补这一空白，为时延储备池计算提供控制理论分析框架。

Method: 引入基于泛函范数的分离性和渐近记忆形式化定义，建立与时延系统增量输入状态稳定性的联系。针对线性储备池，通过傅里叶分析推导分离距离的显式下界，提供可计算的储备池设计准则。在NARMA10基准和连续时间系统预测上进行数值验证。

Result: 建立了储备池计算核心特性的控制理论框架，为线性储备池提供了可计算的分离距离下界。数值实验表明该方法在NARMA10基准和连续时间系统预测上有效，且仅需最小化数字实现。

Conclusion: 本文首次为时延储备池计算提供了严格的数学基础，将储备池特性与控制理论稳定性概念联系起来，为储备池设计提供了理论指导。所提框架为理解储备池计算机制和优化设计开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Reservoir computing is a well-established approach for processing data with a much lower complexity compared to traditional neural networks. Despite two decades of experimental progress, the core properties of reservoir computing (namely separation, robustness, and fading memory) still lack rigorous mathematical foundations. This paper addresses this gap by providing a control-theoretic framework for the analysis of time-delay-based reservoir computers. We introduce formal definitions of the separation property and fading memory in terms of functional norms, and establish their connection to well-known stability notions for time-delay systems as incremental input-to-state stability. For a class of linear reservoirs, we derive an explicit lower bound for the separation distance via Fourier analysis, offering a computable criterion for reservoir design. Numerical results on the NARMA10 benchmark and continuous-time system prediction validate the approach with a minimal digital implementation.

</details>


### [194] [Non-linear in-band interference cancellation on base of conjugate gradients method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18727)
*Alexander Degtyarev,Sergei Bakhurin,Nikita Yudin*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种基于混合牛顿法的共轭梯度方法，用于全双工通信系统中多层非线性自干扰消除器的自适应优化，在收敛速度和计算资源需求之间取得平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全双工通信系统中的自干扰消除问题需要多层非线性模型进行数字域消除。传统二阶方法因海森矩阵不定性而受限，混合牛顿法计算资源需求高，需要一种兼顾收敛速度和计算效率的优化方法。

Method: 提出基于混合牛顿法的共轭梯度方法，利用损失函数的混合二阶导数信息，无需显式计算完整的海森矩阵。该方法结合了混合牛顿法的鞍点处理能力和共轭梯度法的计算效率。

Result: 所提方法在收敛速度上优于一阶方法，同时计算资源需求显著低于传统二阶方法，适用于多层非线性自干扰消除器的自适应优化。

Conclusion: 基于混合牛顿法的共轭梯度方法为全双工通信系统中的自干扰消除提供了一种有效的优化方案，在收敛性能和计算复杂度之间实现了良好平衡。

Abstract: This paper investigates one possible solution to the problem of self-interference cancellation (SIC) arising in the design of in-band full-duplex (IBFD) communication systems. Self-interference cancellation is performed in the digital domain using multilayer nonlinear models adapted via gradient-based optimization. The presence of local minima and saddle points during the adaptation of multilayer models limits the direct use of second-order methods due to the indefiniteness of the hessian matrix. The mixed Newton method can address the saddle-point issue; however, it requires significant computational resources. In this work, a conjugate gradient (CG) method constructed on the base of the mixed Newton method (MNM) is proposed. The method exploits information from mixed second-order derivatives of the loss function without explicit computation the full hessian matrix. As a result, the proposed approach achieves a higher convergence rate than first-order methods while requiring significantly lower computational resources than conventional second-order methods when adapting multilayer nonlinear self-interference cancellers in full-duplex communication systems.

</details>


### [195] [Scheduling Ground-Based Telescope Observations with Uncertain Nights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18730)
*Thomas Rahab Lacroix,Pierre Lemaire,Anne-Marie Lagrange,Julien Milli,Nadia Brauner*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 论文研究基于随机优化和反应策略的天文望远镜观测调度方法，以应对天气不确定性带来的清晰夜晚数量不确定问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地面望远镜观测成本高昂（ESO每晚8.3万欧元），且受天气条件（云层、风、大气湍流）影响，存在清晰夜晚数量的不确定性。需要优化调度以充分利用望远镜时间，自动化工具支持至关重要。

Method: 采用基于随机优化和反应策略的数学方法，考虑多个观测目标、不确定的夜晚数量以及各种观测约束条件，建立调度模型。

Result: 通过与其他方法比较，证明了随机优化和反应策略方法的可行性和有效性。

Conclusion: 在天气和大气条件不确定导致清晰夜晚数量不确定的情况下，基于随机优化和反应策略的调度方法能够显著提高地面望远镜观测效率。

Abstract: The observation of celestial objects is a fundamental activity in astronomy. Ground-based and space telescopes are used to gather electromagnetic radiation from space, allowing astronomers to study a wide range of celestial objects and phenomena, such as stars, planets, galaxies, and black holes. The European Southern Observatory (ESO) charges each night 83 kEUR (Milli et al. 2019), so the schedules of the telescopes are really important in order to optimize every second. Ground-based telescopes are affected by meteorological conditions, such as clouds, wind, and atmospheric turbulence. Accurate scheduling of observations in the presence of such uncertainties can significantly improve the efficiency of telescopes use and support from automated tools is highly desirable. In this paper, we study a mathematical approach for scheduling ground-based telescope observations under an uncertain number of clear nights due to uncertain weather and atmospheric conditions. The model considers multiple targets, uncertain number of nights, and various observing constraints. We demonstrate the viability and effectiveness of an approach based on stochastic optimization and reactive strategy comparing it against other methods.

</details>


### [196] [Second-Order Conditions for Infinite-Horizon Semilinear Parabolic Control Problems without Tikhonov Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18745)
*Eduardo Casas,Nicolai Jork*

Main category: math.OC
